	float mean = 0.0;
	float squared_mean = 0.0;
	float uvStride = samples <= 1.0 ? 0.0 : 2.0 / ( samples - 1.0 );

			squared_mean += distribution.y * distribution.y + distribution.x * distribution.x;

			mean += depth;
			squared_mean += depth * depth;

	squared_mean = squared_mean / samples;

var T_e = `
void main() {

	gl_Position = vec4( position, 1.0 );

}`;
var B_e = `
uniform sampler2DArray depthColor;
uniform float depthWidth;
uniform float depthHeight;

void main() {

	vec2 coord = vec2( gl_FragCoord.x / depthWidth, gl_FragCoord.y / depthHeight );

	if ( coord.x >= 1.0 ) {

		gl_FragDepth = texture( depthColor, vec3( coord.x - 1.0, coord.y, 1 ) ).r;

	} else {

		gl_FragDepth = texture( depthColor, vec3( coord.x, coord.y, 0 ) ).r;

	}

}`;
var M_e = class {
  constructor() {
    this.texture = null;
    this.mesh = null;
    this.depthNear = 0;
    this.depthFar = 0;
  }
  init(e, t) {
    if (this.texture === null) {
      const n = new EF(e.texture);
      (e.depthNear !== t.depthNear || e.depthFar !== t.depthFar) && (this.depthNear = e.depthNear, this.depthFar = e.depthFar);
      this.texture = n;
    }
  }
  getMesh(e) {
    if (this.texture !== null && this.mesh === null) {
      const t = e.cameras[0].viewport;
      const n = new Pp({
        vertexShader: T_e,
        fragmentShader: B_e,
        uniforms: {
          depthColor: {
            value: this.texture
          },
          depthWidth: {
            value: t.z
          },
          depthHeight: {
            value: t.w
          }
        }
      });
      this.mesh = new nr(new xm(20, 20), n);
    }
    return this.mesh;
  }
  reset() {
    this.texture = null;
    this.mesh = null;
  }
  getDepthTexture() {
    return this.texture;
  }
};

          }
        }
      });

    };

    function ar(Te, gt, xt, At) {
      if (Te.visible === !1) {
        return;
      }
      if (Te.layers.test(gt.layers)) {
        if (Te.isGroup) {
          xt = Te.renderOrder;
        } else {
          if (Te.isLOD) {
            Te.autoUpdate === !0 && Te.update(gt);
          } else {
            if (Te.isLightProbeGrid) {
              D.pushLightProbeGrid(Te);
            } else {
              if (Te.isLight) {
                D.pushLight(Te);
                Te.castShadow && D.pushShadow(Te);
              } else {
                if (Te.isSprite) {
                  if (!Te.frustumCulled || Ke.intersectsSprite(Te)) {
                    At && et.setFromMatrixPosition(Te.matrixWorld).applyMatrix4(Be);
                    const vn = ie.update(Te);
                    const In = Te.material;
                    In.visible && R.push(Te, vn, In, xt, et.z, null);
                  }
                } else {
                  if ((Te.isMesh || Te.isLine || Te.isPoints) && (!Te.frustumCulled || Ke.intersectsObject(Te))) {
                    const vn = ie.update(Te);
                    const In = Te.material;
                    At && (Te.boundingSphere !== void 0 ? (Te.boundingSphere === null && Te.computeBoundingSphere(), et.copy(Te.boundingSphere.center)) : (vn.boundingSphere === null && vn.computeBoundingSphere(), et.copy(vn.boundingSphere.center)), et.applyMatrix4(Te.matrixWorld).applyMatrix4(Be));
                    if (Array.isArray(In)) {
                      const yn = vn.groups;
                      for (let zn = 0, ei = yn.length; zn < ei; zn++) {
                        const Ee = yn[zn];
                        const Ce = In[Ee.materialIndex];
                        Ce && Ce.visible && R.push(Te, vn, Ce, xt, et.z, Ee);
                      }
                    } else {
                      In.visible && R.push(Te, vn, In, xt, et.z, null);
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
      const vt = Te.children;
      for (let vn = 0, In = vt.length; vn < In; vn++) {
        ar(vt[vn], gt, xt, At);
      }
    }
    function Xr(Te, gt, xt, At) {

    }
    function $i(Te, gt, xt, At) {

      _e.updateRenderTargetMipmap(vt);
      if (Zt.has("WEBGL_multisampled_render_to_texture") === !1) {
        let Ce = !1;
        for (let We = 0, tt = gt.length; We < tt; We++) {
          const {
            object: ot,
            geometry: Pt,
            material: zt,
